		}

				is_claimed_by = ROOT
			}
		}
		add_claim_by = ROOT
	}
	# Issue one goal per current eligible owner after adding the claims.
	every_country = {
		limit = {
			exists = yes
			has_capitulated = no
			NOT = {
				tag = ROOT
				is_in_faction_with = ROOT
				is_subject_of = ROOT
				has_non_aggression_pact_with = ROOT
				has_war_with = ROOT
			}
			any_owned_state = {
				WW1_roman_states_west = yes
				is_claimed_by = ROOT
				is_controlled_by = PREV
			}
			ROOT = { NOT = { has_wargoal_against = { target = PREV type = take_claimed_state } } }
		}
		ROOT = {
			create_wargoal = { type = take_claimed_state target = PREV }
		}
	}
}

endsieg_fixed_effect_eadc5e5be083d517 = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			country_exists = FROM
			NOT = { tag = FROM }
			NOT = { is_in_array = { array = endsieg_focus_opinion_offered_support value = FROM } }
		}
		add_opinion_modifier = { target = FROM modifier = offered_support }
		hidden_effect = {
			add_to_array = { endsieg_focus_opinion_offered_support = FROM }
		}
	}
}

		limit = { country_exists = SHA NOT = { tag = SHA } }
		set_temp_variable = { endsieg_opinion_partner = SHA }
		var:endsieg_opinion_partner = {
			endsieg_fixed_effect_49d234fc29cff99f = yes
		}
	}
}
